SECTION 11: PLATFORM NUMBER RULES IN SHARED CODE (V3)
=================================================================
  Shared code turning a number into text or text into a number with KOTLIN's rules. On the iPad these differ from Android's
  Java rules (digits written; text accepted). Anything stored, hashed, synced or shown must use GtJava.jd / GtJavaParse.
  (Int/Long toString is the same on both platforms; review each line -- SQL arguments are value-safe.)
